### gap-3-unaudited-mid-size-fork-hunks-in-upstrea#5 [MINOR/correctness/rewrite] server-utils post-emit regex rewriting of import specifiers: matches inside strings/comments, invalidates sourcemaps, misses .mts/.cts, and leaks a temp config into the user's app root on failure

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/server/utils/src/compilers/typescript/index.ts:21-22`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/server/utils/src/compilers/typescript/index.ts:170-216`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/server/utils/src/compilers/typescript/index.ts:148-160`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/server/utils/src/compilers/typescript/index.ts:84-95`

**Evidence:** The old upstream alias rewrite was a TS AST `before` transformer (deleted from tsconfigPathsPlugin.ts), so emit and sourcemaps stayed consistent. The fork rewrites emitted .js with `importSpecifierRE = /((?:from\s*|import\s*\(\s*|require\s*\(\s*)['"])([^'"]+)(['"])/g` (index.ts:21-22) applied to whole file contents (rewriteOutputSpecifiers, :170-216): it also matches `from '...'`/`require('...')` text inside string literals and emitted comments, and rewritten specifiers change column offsets without regenerating .js.map files, so any app tsconfig with sourceMap:true ships stale maps for the BFF dist. getSourceFileForOutput (:148-160) only probes .ts/.tsx/.js/.jsx so .mts/.cts sources are never rewritten. Separately, createResolvedTsgoConfig writes `.tsgo.${process.pid}.resolved.json` into appDirectory (:84-95) with no try/finally — a spawn 'error' rejection skips the fs.remove at :281, leaving the temp file in the user's project; the pid-based name also collides if two compiles (plugin-bff cli.ts:97 and app-tools serverBuild.ts:64) ever overlap in one process; and the code comment claims the file is kept 'beside the app tsconfig' while it is actually written to appDirectory, mis-basing relative `files` entries when server.tsconfigPath points into a subdirectory.

**Direction:** Wrap the resolved-config lifecycle in try/finally with a unique (pid+counter or mkdtemp) name placed in path.dirname(tsconfigPath) to match the comment and the --showConfig base. For the rewriter, either anchor matches to statement positions via a real parse (the repo already vendors oxc via code-tools' oxlint stack, or use es-module-lexer for import/export specifiers) or at minimum drop/regenerate .map files when a file is rewritten; add .mts/.cts to source probing.

**Verifier:** All claims verify against src/compilers/typescript/index.ts (a fork rewrite; baseline tsconfigPathsPlugin.ts confirmed to have used a TS AST visitNode transformer, now reduced 124→12 lines). importSpecifierRE (actual lines 23-24, cited as 21-22 — immaterial) is applied to whole emitted-file contents in rewriteOutputSpecifiers (:180-230), so quoted text after `from`/`import(`/`require(` inside string literals or comments can match; no `.map` handling exists anywhere in the file, so rewrites with changed specifier lengths leave stale sourcemaps when the app tsconfig emits them; getSourceFileForOutput (:160-175) probes only .ts/.tsx/.js/.jsx; createResolvedTsgoConfig writes `.tsgo.${pid}.resolved.json` into appDirectory (:87-90) under a comment claiming 'beside the app tsconfig' (:85-86), and the only fs.remove (:276) is skipped if runTsgo rejects via the spawn 'error' handler (:140) — no try/finally. The pid-collision claim is plausible but speculative (plugin-bff and app-tools serverBuild compiles likely run sequentially); treat it as hardening, not an observed bug. Severity minor is right. Proposed direction is fork-local; note the rewrite path doesn't exist in vanilla so no upstream-merge impact.

### gap-3-unaudited-mid-size-fork-hunks-in-upstrea#6 [MINOR/upstream-compat/small-fix] server/server path-to-regexp ^6→^8 (+minimatch 3→10) is a coupled dependency migration with user-visible mock-syntax breakage, but the ledger tags the package [M] mostly-mechanical

Add a mock fixture with a `:param`-style key and one asserting the v8 behavior for previously-valid v6 syntax (clear error or documented migration). Mention the mock-pattern syntax change in the fork docs/changelog.

**Verifier:** Core claims verified, including empirically: fork diff bumps path-to-regexp ^6.3.0→^8.4.2 and minimatch ^3.1.2→^10.2.5 and drops `encode: encodeURI` from getMatched (mock.ts); running the repo's installed path-to-regexp@8.4.2 confirms `:id?`, `:id(\d+)`, and `(.*)` all throw at parse time ('Unexpected ? / ( at index ...'), while plain `:id` works — so v6-syntax mock keys crash per-request inside getMatched's find loop; all mock fixtures (packages/server/server/tests/fixtures/*/config/mock) use only literal paths, so nothing covers it. One correction: the FORK-DIVERGENCE 'server (16 files) — [M]' entry does more than footnote — it explicitly says 'on conflict in this file keep the fork side ... do not assume the whole package is take-upstream-safe' for mock.ts, so the sync hazard is partially mitigated; what is genuinely missing is (a) flagging package.json's two-major dep bump as a coupled migration like the plugin-polyfill entry and (b) any mention of the user-visible v6 mock-pattern breakage. ### gap-3-unaudited-mid-size-fork-hunks-in-upstrea#7 [MINOR/testing/small-fix] Re-enabled builder e2e dev tests run on every PR, but dev.https and tools.devServer were de-flaked by making their core assertions unfalsifiable

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/tests/e2e/builder/cases/dev/dev.test.ts:152-181`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/tests/e2e/builder/cases/dev/dev.test.ts:183-237`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/.github/workflows/test-builder-e2e.yml`

**Evidence:** Fork commit 80e93a5bbf removed the upstream test.skip's; test-builder-e2e.yml runs the suite on pull_request to main and merge_group, so they are live CI. The two HMR tests are genuinely strengthened (random ports, polling expects with 30s timeouts, finally-cleanup). But dev.https (dev.test.ts:152-181) catches any error and passes if the message matches /(devcert|certificate|https|openssl|self-signed)/i — 'https' appears in essentially any failure from an https dev-server flow, so a real regression still goes green; the only positive assertion (`urls[0].startsWith('https')`) is unreachable on machines without devcert. tools.devServer (:183-237) wraps its entire behavioral assertion in `if (setupCalled && reloadFn) {...}` — if setupMiddlewares is never invoked (the regression the test exists to catch), the test passes having only verified a page renders.

**Direction:** In dev.https, detect devcert availability up front and `test.skip(!hasDevcert, ...)` explicitly, then keep only the positive https-URL assertion; remove the message-regex catch. In tools.devServer, replace the conditional with `expect(setupCalled).toBe(true)` and a hard assertion that reloadFn ran.

**Verifier:** Verified. Baseline 8a744c1b31 had test.skip on all four tests (lines 9, 100, 147, 166 of the old file); fork commit 80e93a5bbf removed the skips; .github/workflows/test-builder-e2e.yml triggers on pull_request→main and merge_group and runs `pnpm run test:builder`, so they are live CI. dev.test.ts:153-182 — the catch asserts message.toLowerCase() matches /(devcert|certificate|https|openssl|self-signed)/, and any dev-server failure in an https flow plausibly contains 'https', so realistic regressions go green; the sole positive assertion is unreachable without devcert. tools.devServer (:184-237) wraps reload verification in `if (setupCalled && reloadFn)`, so a setupMiddlewares regression passes with only a render check. One nuance overstating 'unfalsifiable': an in-try assertion failure (toBeTruthy message lacks the keywords) WOULD still fail the test — but the realistic crash-regression path is indeed swallowed. Proposed fixes are fork-local test changes; no vanilla/merge impact (upstream keeps these skipped). Minor is correct.

### gap-3-unaudited-mid-size-fork-hunks-in-upstrea#8 [MINOR/dx/refactor] @modern-js/code-tools hardcodes the en/cs locale convention and exact import identifiers into a generically-named published guardrail package

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/toolkit/code-tools/src/cli/workspace-source-check.ts:58-61`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/toolkit/code-tools/src/cli/workspace-source-check.ts:67-93`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/toolkit/code-tools/src/cli/workspace-source-check.ts:116-118`

**Evidence:** The package is otherwise healthy (the template scripts correctly delegate instead of duplicating; the oxlint plugin/CLI plumbing is solid), explaining the prior zero-findings result. But the workspace gate bakes in one project's i18n convention: isLocaleJson only matches `/locales/(en|cs)/` (workspace-source-check.ts:58-61), so a workspace adding de/fr locale files silently bypasses plural validation entirely; checkRuntimeResources (:67-93) requires the literal identifiers `csResource`/`enResource` and a `resources,` shorthand-property regex in modern.runtime.ts, so renaming a variable or writing `resources: resources` fails the gate despite correct behavior; Czech plural categories are hardcoded (:116-118). All of this ships in a package named '@modern-js/code-tools — Code analysis tooling for Modern.js projects' with no configuration surface (runWorkspaceSourceCheck accepts only cwd/sourceRoots).

**Direction:** Lift locales (and per-locale plural categories via Intl.PluralRules(locale).resolvedOptions or CLDR data) plus the expected-resource-import contract into WorkspaceSourceCheckOptions, and have the create generator emit them into the workspace's check script from its locale configuration. Replace the identifier-literal regexes with a check that locale JSONs under each configured locale are imported and passed to initOptions.resources, regardless of variable names.

**Verifier:** Verified; the whole package is fork-added (all A in baseline diff). workspace-source-check.ts:58-61 matches only /locales/(en|cs)/ so other locales silently bypass plural validation; :76-82 requires the literal identifiers csResource/enResource; :117-118 hardcodes Czech plural categories; WorkspaceSourceCheckOptions (:5-8) exposes only cwd/sourceRoots. One evidence correction: the claim that `resources: resources` fails the gate is wrong — the `\bresources\s*,` regex (:86) matches the value identifier followed by a comma in `resources: resources,`, so that exact form passes; however a renamed variable (`resources: localeResources`) or inline object does fail, so the rigidity claim stands in substance. Core finding (one project's i18n convention baked into a generically-named published package with no configuration surface) is accurate; the rest of the package being healthy also checks out (template scripts delegate, oxlint plumbing is self-contained). Proposed direction is fork-local; severity minor is right.

## gap-4-dependency-supply-chain-fork-authored-mo  (5 confirmed)

The dependency surface is functionally consistent today (pnpm 11.5.3 pinned identically in .mise.toml/packageManager/engines; allowBuilds verified complete via `pnpm ignored-builds` reporting None; minimumReleaseAgeExclude is live config against pnpm 11's verified 24h default; .pnpmfile.cjs unchanged from upstream; renovate has no automerge; scripts/{build,prebundle,release-note,lint-package-json} mods are mechanical). The structural problem is that the entire dependency-management lane — two dist-level MF patches, a 30-entry overrides block, and the renovate policy — is completely undocumented: FORK-DIVERGENCE.md scopes itself to `packages/` only, no ADR or commit message explains the patches, and the patch filenames don't even match the version they're applied to. The patches also only exist inside the monorepo, so the fork's MF contract gates validate patched @module-federation code that create-generated consumer apps (pinned to the same 2.5.1) never receive. Renovate's weekly grouped minor/patch PRs will collide head-on with the exact-pin lattice (patchedDependencies keys, exact react-router overrides, exact-version minimumReleaseAgeExclude entries) with no carve-out packageRules.

### gap-4-dependency-supply-chain-fork-authored-mo#1 [MAJOR/upstream-compat/rewrite] MF dist patches are undocumented, stale-named, not upstreamed, and hard-fail the next MF bump

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/patches/@module-federation__manifest@2.5.0.patch`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/patches/@module-federation__rspack@2.5.0.patch`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/pnpm-workspace.yaml:43-45`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/FORK-DIVERGENCE.md:11`

**Evidence:** Both patches rewrite the BUNDLED dist output (dist/utils.{js,mjs}, dist/ModuleFederationPlugin.{js,mjs}) of @module-federation/manifest and @module-federation/rspack to lazy-require @module-federation/dts-plugin (createRequire in the .mjs builds, plus an early return from getTypesMetaInfo when pluginOptions.dts === false). Filenames say @2.5.0 but pnpm-workspace.yaml:44-45 applies them to '@module-federation/manifest@2.5.1'/'@module-federation/rspack@2.5.1' (the rename commit 138c381395 'chore: update module federation to 2.5.0' shows the files are carried forward across versions with 0 content change; the 2.5.0->2.5.1 bump didn't even rename them). Rationale exists nowhere: FORK-DIVERGENCE.md explicitly regenerates from `git diff ... -- packages` so patches/ and pnpm-workspace.yaml are outside the ledger (zero grep hits for 'patch|module-federation' in it), docs/super-app-rfc-adr has zero hits for patchedDependencies/dts-plugin, and the originating commit 4b48676e14 is a giant 'chore(ultramodern): update workspace dependencies' with no body. npm registry shows @module-federation/rspack latest is still 2.5.1 (2026-06-04), so the fix is not upstreamed and no upstream issue is referenced. pnpm 11.5.3 defaults allowUnusedPatches:false (verified in pnpm dist: UNUSED_PATCH is an error), so any MF version bump — which .github/renovate.json will propose weekly inside its grouped 'npm minor and patch updates' PR — makes `pnpm install` hard-fail until someone regenerates patches against new webpack-concatenated dist output, which rarely re-applies cleanly across versions.

**Direction:** Rename the patch files to @2.5.1 to match the keys; add a patches/README.md (or FORK-DIVERGENCE.md appendix) recording the exact failure the patches fix and the regeneration procedure (`pnpm patch @module-federation/rspack@<v>`); file/link an upstream module-federation issue so the patch has an exit criterion; add a renovate packageRule that splits @module-federation/* into its own PR group (or excludes it) with a comment pointing at patchedDependencies, so an MF bump cannot poison the whole weekly group.

**Verifier:** All evidence verifies. Both patch files (A in baseline diff) rewrite bundled dist of @module-federation/{manifest,rspack} to lazy-require dts-plugin (createRequire in .mjs) plus early-return from getTypesMetaInfo when pluginOptions.dts===false — confirmed by reading patches/. pnpm-workspace.yaml:43-45 keys are @2.5.1 while filenames say @2.5.0 (commit 138c381395 renamed the files across versions with 0 content change). FORK-DIVERGENCE.md regenerates from `git diff ... -- packages` and has zero hits for patch/module-federation/pnpm-workspace; docs/super-app-rfc-adr has zero hits for patchedDependencies/dts-plugin. npm registry confirms @module-federation/rspack and manifest latest = 2.5.1 (modified 2026-06-04), so not upstreamed. pnpm 11.5.3 dist confirms default allowUnusedPatches:false and the UNUSED_PATCH error ('The following patches were not used'), so any MF bump (which renovate's weekly grouped npm PR would propose) hard-fails install. Proposed direction is safe — pure docs/renovate-rule additions, no vanilla-behavior or merge impact (patches/ and renovate.json don't exist upstream).

### gap-4-dependency-supply-chain-fork-authored-mo#2 [MAJOR/correctness/rewrite] MF patches apply only in-monorepo: contract gates test patched MF that create-generated consumers never get

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/toolkit/create/template-workspace/pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/toolkit/create/src/ultramodern-workspace/versions.ts:7`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/packages/toolkit/create/src/ultramodern-workspace/package-json.ts:141-143`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/pnpm-workspace.yaml:43-45`

**Evidence:** pnpm patchedDependencies only affect installs of THIS workspace. No published workspace package depends on @module-federation/* (lockfile importers show MF deps only under tests/integration/i18n/mf/*, tests/integration/routes-tanstack-mf/*, tests/integration/write-to-dist) — so the patches exist solely so the fork's own MF integration fixtures build, and the MF contract gates (validate:mv-topology-smoke runs routes-tanstack-mf tests) certify behavior of a patched @module-federation/rspack+manifest. Meanwhile create pins generated apps to the exact same versions unpatched: versions.ts:7 MODULE_FEDERATION_VERSION='2.5.1', package-json.ts:141-143 emits @module-federation/{bridge-react,modern-js-v3,runtime}, and template-workspace/pnpm-workspace.yaml contains no patchedDependencies and the template ships no patches/ dir. If the lazy dts-plugin require fixes a real crash (e.g. ESM import of '@module-federation/dts-plugin/core' or a missing `typescript` peer in the tsgo-first lane — dts-plugin@2.5.1 declares typescript ^4.9||^5 as a required peer per pnpm-lock.yaml:7396, and the fork generates MF configs with dts compilerInstance 'tsgo' at create/src/ultramodern-workspace/module-federation.ts:404,479), consumer apps hit the unpatched failure that fork CI can never reproduce.

**Direction:** Reproduce the original failure with the patches removed: if it no longer reproduces on MF 2.5.1, delete both patches and the patchedDependencies block; if it does reproduce, replicate the patchedDependencies + patches/ into the create template-workspace output (and document it), or move the workaround into fork-owned plugin code (e.g. force dts:false handling before MF plugin instantiation) so consumers and CI run the same code.

**Verifier:** Core claim verifies. pnpm patchedDependencies are workspace-local; sweep confirms no packages/*/package.json depends on @module-federation/* (only tests/integration fixtures), so the patched MF exists solely for the fork's own fixtures and gates (validate:mv-topology-smoke in root package.json:56 runs routes-tanstack-mf/tests/tanstack-mf-contract.test.ts). create pins generated apps to the identical unpatched versions: versions.ts:7 MODULE_FEDERATION_VERSION='2.5.1', package-json.ts:141-143 emits the three MF deps, and template-workspace/pnpm-workspace.yaml has no patchedDependencies and ships no patches/. dts-plugin@2.5.1 typescript peer (^4.9||^5, non-optional) confirmed at pnpm-lock.yaml:7396; module-federation.ts ~404/479 emit dts compilerInstance 'tsgo'. One correction: generated configs ENABLE dts, so the dts===false early-return is not the consumer-relevant path — the eager-vs-lazy dts-plugin module load (and its typescript peer at load time) is the divergence consumers would hit. The conditional framing ('if the patch fixes a real crash') is properly hedged. Proposed direction (reproduce, then delete or replicate/move into fork plugin code) is sound and fork-local. Fork-owned: patches and create generator are fork-added.

### gap-4-dependency-supply-chain-fork-authored-mo#3 [MAJOR/upstream-compat/refactor] Renovate weekly grouped minor/patch updates collide with the fork's exact-pin lattice with no carve-outs

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/.github/renovate.json:17-22`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/pnpm-workspace.yaml:43-45`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/pnpm-workspace.yaml:109-159`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/pnpm-workspace.yaml:51-52`

**Evidence:** renovate.json groups ALL npm patch+minor updates into one weekly PR with rangeStrategy 'bump'. At least four exact-pin mechanisms guarantee that PR fails: (1) patchedDependencies keys pinned to @2.5.1 hard-fail install on any MF bump (pnpm 11 allowUnusedPatches:false, verified); (2) overrides pin react-router/react-router-dom to exact 7.17.0 (pnpm-workspace.yaml:51-52) while packages declare ranges (plugin-ssg peer '>=7.13.1'), so a react-router bump silently does nothing until the override is hand-edited in a file renovate may not manage; (3) minimumReleaseAgeExclude (pnpm-workspace.yaml:109-159) lists ~50 EXACT versions (e.g. '@tanstack/react-router@1.170.15', '@types/node@25.9.3', 26 sharp/@img platform pins at 0.35.0/1.3.0) — pnpm 11 defaults minimum-release-age to 24*60 minutes (verified in pnpm 11.5.3 dist), so any renovate bump to a <24h-old version of these fails resolution until a new exact entry is added, which is exactly the churn pattern that produced the current 50-entry list; (4) the already-confirmed exact-source-text publish/contract gates and the contract-doctor/create TanStack pins (1.170.8 vs 1.170.15 drift) brick on any TanStack bump. One failing dep poisons the entire grouped PR every Monday. Mitigations present: dependencyDashboardApproval for majors, no automerge anywhere (verified in both renovate.json files), minimumReleaseAge '1 day' enforced by scripts/security/validate-github-workflows.mjs:68.

**Direction:** Add renovate packageRules that (a) put @module-federation/*, @tanstack/*, react-router* and other gate-pinned packages into separate, individually-failing PR groups or disable them with a pointer to the gate/pin that must move in lockstep; (b) in pnpm-workspace.yaml, convert minimumReleaseAgeExclude entries from exact versions to bare package names (the intent is 'trust this publisher's fresh releases', not 'trust exactly this version') — the template-workspace already does this correctly with unversioned entries.

**Verifier:** Kept with one correction. Verified: renovate.json (fork-added) groups all npm patch+minor into one weekly PR with rangeStrategy bump and no packageRules carve-outs; no automerge in either renovate.json; majors gated by dependencyDashboardApproval; scripts/security/validate-github-workflows.mjs enforces minimumReleaseAge '1 day'. Mechanism (1) verified: pnpm 11.5.3 defaults allowUnusedPatches:false (UNUSED_PATCH error in dist), so any MF bump breaks install for the whole grouped PR. Mechanism (2) verified: overrides pin react-router/react-router-dom 7.17.0 (pnpm-workspace.yaml:51-52) vs plugin-ssg peer react-router-dom '>=7.13.1' — bumps silently no-op. Mechanism (4) relies on already-confirmed gate/pin findings (contract-doctor carries 1.170.8, versions.ts 1.170.15 — both confirmed present). CORRECTION on mechanism (3): renovate's own minimumReleaseAge '1 day' aligns with pnpm's default 24*60 floor (confirmed '"minimum-release-age": 24 * 60' in pnpm 11.5.3 dist), so renovate never proposes <24h-old versions — the exact-version minimumReleaseAgeExclude entries go stale/dead on bumps rather than fail resolution; that sub-claim is overstated. Also proposed direction (b) (bare package names in the exclude list) permanently disables the release-age gate for those publishers, weakening the supply-chain protection the exact one-shot entries provide — keep direction (a), reconsider (b).

### gap-4-dependency-supply-chain-fork-authored-mo#4 [MINOR/hygiene/small-fix] Dependency-management surface is a FORK-DIVERGENCE.md blind spot; autoInstallPeers silently flipped true->false in the '.npmrc migration'

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/FORK-DIVERGENCE.md:9-11`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/pnpm-workspace.yaml:23-25`, `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/.npmrc`

**Evidence:** FORK-DIVERGENCE.md regenerates its ledger from `git diff ... -- packages`, so patches/, pnpm-workspace.yaml (+138 lines of fork policy), .pnpmfile.cjs interactions, renovate.json and root package.json are structurally invisible to the upstream-sync playbook even though they are merge-conflict-prone (upstream also edits pnpm-workspace.yaml/.npmrc/package.json). Within that blind spot, the .npmrc->workspace.yaml migration is NOT semantically neutral: link-workspace-packages=false and strict-peer-dependencies=false moved over equivalently, but the fork additionally set autoInstallPeers:false — baseline pnpm-lock.yaml settings show autoInstallPeers:true, current shows false. That flips peer-resolution behavior monorepo-wide (presumably to stop `typescript` auto-installing in the tsgo lane, cf. peerDependencyRules.allowedVersions '@module-federation/*>typescript: >=6 <8') and is documented nowhere.

**Direction:** Extend FORK-DIVERGENCE.md with a short 'root/infra' section covering pnpm-workspace.yaml (esp. the autoInstallPeers flip and its tsgo rationale), patches/, and renovate.json; or add YAML comments next to the settings themselves so the rationale survives upstream merges.

**Verifier:** Verifies. FORK-DIVERGENCE.md's regenerate command is `git diff ... --name-status -M -- packages`, structurally excluding patches/, pnpm-workspace.yaml, renovate.json, .pnpmfile.cjs and root package.json (file has zero hits for patch/module-federation/pnpm-workspace). Migration check: baseline .npmrc had link-workspace-packages=false and strict-peer-dependencies=false, both moved equivalently to pnpm-workspace.yaml:24-25; but autoInstallPeers:false (line 23) is a new fork addition — baseline pnpm-lock.yaml settings show autoInstallPeers:true, current shows false — a monorepo-wide peer-resolution behavior flip with no comment or doc anywhere (the only YAML comments cover the peerDependencyRules TS lanes). Note the migration itself was forced: pnpm 11 no longer reads the package.json 'pnpm' field, so documenting (not reverting) is the right direction; the proposed doc-only fix has no merge or behavior impact. Severity minor is right.

### gap-4-dependency-supply-chain-fork-authored-mo#5 [MINOR/dx/small-fix] .vscode/settings.json fork hunk injects Prettier/ESLint settings (with a duplicate key) into a Biome-only repo

**Files:** `/Users/satan/side/experiments/modernjs/.vscode/settings.json:31-51`

**Evidence:** The fork hunk keeps the upstream 'editor.defaultFormatter': 'biomejs.biome' and then adds a SECOND 'editor.defaultFormatter': 'esbenp.prettier-vscode' in the same object (later key wins in VS Code's JSONC), and replaces 'quickfix.biome' codeActionsOnSave with 'source.fixAll.eslint'. The repo has no ESLint or Prettier anywhere: biome.json is the formatter/linter, root package.json lint-staged runs `biome check`, and neither prettier nor eslint appear in root devDependencies. Contributors' editors will format with an unconfigured Prettier and run no Biome fixes, fighting lint-staged on every commit. This reads as an accidental paste from another project. ('typescript.tsdk': 'node_modules/typescript/lib' also survives despite the tsgo-first toolchain.)

**Direction:** Restore the upstream block: single 'editor.defaultFormatter': 'biomejs.biome' (Biome 2.x action name 'source.fixAll.biome' or 'quickfix.biome'), delete the prettier/eslint/json-format keys.

**Verifier:** Core claim verifies and the hunk is fork-owned: git diff 8a744c1b31..HEAD confirms the fork replaced global codeActionsOnSave 'quickfix.biome' with 'source.fixAll.eslint' and added a second top-level 'editor.defaultFormatter': 'esbenp.prettier-vscode' (duplicate of upstream's biomejs.biome at line 31; later key wins) plus [json]/[jsonc] blocks that duplicate upstream's later biome-pinned [json]/[jsonc] blocks. Repo is indeed Biome-only (lint-staged runs biome check; no eslint/prettier in devDependencies). 'Accidental paste' reading is fair. IMPACT CORRECTION: upstream's per-language blocks ([typescript], [typescriptreact], [javascript], [javascriptreact], [json], [jsonc]) survive unchanged and override globals in VS Code, so TS/JS/JSON/JSONC — essentially the whole lint-staged surface — still format with Biome and run quickfix.biome on save; 'contributors fight lint-staged on every commit' is overstated. Damage is confined to global-scope file types (md/css/yaml) and the dead eslint action. Also 'typescript.tsdk' is upstream's pre-existing line, not part of the fork hunk. Proposed restore-upstream direction is correct and merge-friendly. Minor stands.

Rejected during verification: Dead '@remix-run/router' override — zero resolutions in the lockfile since react-router was forced to v7; Test orchestrator floats `npm exec pnpm@11` outside the fork's own engines window (confirms prior flag, adds engines contradiction)
